# Done queue items — archived from docs/OPEN.md (2026-10)

Historical record, not a work list. Every item here was ticked [x] in
docs/OPEN.md and was moved verbatim by `node scripts/archive-done.mjs --write`
(run by `npm run inventories:refresh`; docs/OPEN.md Q16). The queue tools still
read this file with docs/OPEN.md: the score line and next free number
(scripts/queue-count.mjs), the Everything-open block (scripts/scoreboard.mjs)
and src/test/queueItemsNameTheirGuard.test.ts. To REOPEN an item, move its
block back into docs/OPEN.md and untick it (a number in both files fails the
duplicate-number check).

## Archived 2026-10-01 — from "FEEDS — mirrored from the alert ledger, nightly-red issues and the audit bus (node scripts/open-sync-trackers.mjs)"

- [x] **Q888 refresh bot force-pushed over a person's fix on its PR.** 2026-09-30: `.github/actions/refresh-pr` rebuilt bot/refresh/<id> from main and force-pushed it every run, so 90fa25368 (a human fix on bot/refresh/loading-states, PR #1932) was wiped by the bot's 926ebcbe0. Fixed: every bot-branch push now goes through `scripts/ci/bot-branch-push.sh`, which replays non-bot commits onto main with the fresh measurement on top, refuses (exit 3, `::error::` + PR comment naming the commits, nothing pushed) on any conflict or hand-edited merge, and pushes only with `--force-with-lease=<ref>:<sha it read>`; the no-change path rebases instead of closing and deleting a branch that carries a person's commit. Guard: `src/test/botBranchPushKeepsHumanCommits.test.ts` (no direct git push in any workflow/action YAML; lease-only pushes; 9 behaviour cases against a throwaway git repo) — red 11/11 on main, vacuity 6/6 killed.

  sign-out followed by sign-in in the same session never re-saved the token.

## Archived 2026-10-01 — from "QUEUE (cont.) — carried; the retired audit-bus section's table (2026-09-23) is superseded by the FEEDS mirror above"

- [x] **Q803 accept_application never stamps helper_confirmed_at, so "I'm On My Way" dead-ends on every apply→accept hire.** Merged 2026-09-27 from docs/audit/launch-2026-09/inbox/accept-application-never-stamps-helper-confirmed-at.md (deleted: OPEN.md is the one list). Re-verified on prod 2026-09-27 via `pg_get_functiondef`: accept_application never references helper_confirmed_at, helper_mark_on_the_way raises when it is NULL, and only claim_series_dates, respond_to_direct_offer and rpc_group_member_confirm stamp it. Fix: stamp it in accept_application's jobs UPDATE (or gate on `COALESCE(helper_confirmed_at, helper_dayof_confirmed_at)`), and have the E2E journey drive `helper_mark_on_the_way` instead of PATCHing the row. Re-measure: hire via apply→accept, tap "I'm On My Way", see the stamp land. STATUS 2026-09-27 (lane O3): PREMISE REFUTED, prescribed fix withdrawn (its done-when demanded it and is removed). The stamp is missing from accept_application BY DESIGN: accept_application makes the OFFER (status accepted, helper_confirmed_at NULL, response_deadline set); the helper's "Accept Job" tap stamps helper_confirmed_at (useOfferHandlers.ts client UPDATE, allowed by enforce_confirm_on_live_job + the helper column whitelist); expire_unanswered_offers reopens offers still unstamped at the deadline and auto_start_due_jobs starts only stamped ones. Stamping at accept would skip helper consent and disable offer expiry. Measured: live md5 accept_application 4524d9c68b84462743faf7eecbe8c634 (proacl postgres/authenticated/service_role), helper_mark_on_the_way a37e8d9b5d33e3834c9d4e2c335394ee; rolled-back prod probe on is_seed jobs (poster-e2e/helper-e2e): accept1=ok, accept2=ok, A helper confirm UPDATE rows=1 then helper_mark_on_the_way ok (A ends in_progress, confirmed, otw set); B unconfirmed -> helper_not_confirmed; 0 probe rows left. UI: an accepted-but-unconfirmed hire routes to OfferedActions (Accept/Decline), not the tracker, so no "I'm On My Way" is offered there; guard src/pages/jobs/appliedJobCard/appliedJobCardHelpers.test.ts, vacuity 2/2 killed 2026-09-27. Layers read: RPC (live defs), trigger/constraint (enforce_confirm_on_live_job, helper whitelist, expire/auto_start), client write (useOfferHandlers), reader (appliedJobCardHelpers, HelperTrackerPanel, JobTracking), deployed (live md5). OPEN RESIDUAL (why this stays [ ]): the 25/25 evidence came from e2e/prod-lifecycle.spec.ts (~line 908) PATCHing helper_on_the_way_at + status in_progress straight onto the row as the helper, and prod accepted it without helper_confirmed_at: 50 jobs have otw set and confirm NULL (all is_seed, latest 2026-09-26 14:14Z). So the confirm gate lives only in helper_mark_on_the_way and a direct REST PATCH bypasses it. Next: drive the confirm UPDATE + helper_mark_on_the_way in the spec, and decide whether helper_on_the_way_at should leave the helper whitelist (RPC-only), with a guard proven red. **OBSOLETE 2026-10-01: accept flow no longer gates On My Way on helper_confirmed_at (verifier re-read).**

## Archived 2026-10-02 — from "NEW — user-facing: Not Now on push prompt tells user notifications are off"

- [x] **DONE 2026-10-02:** 'Not Now' in our own push rationale no longer toasts "Notifications are off" when the browser/OS was already denied. Root cause was the signal, not the predicate: useRequestPushPermission returned a bare boolean, so NotificationPanel.enablePush could not tell a dismissal from a refusal. New useRequestPushPermissionOutcome (src/lib/nativePush.ts) returns granted | refused | dismissed (dismissed = the rationale callback never ran); the panel toasts only on refused. press-every-control no longer excuses that toast after Not Now (it now fails the press). Layers read: client hook, rationale hook, panel caller, press harness; no server layer. GUARD: src/lib/nativePush.requestOutcome.test.ts (2 @mutate) + src/test/pressPermissionRefusal.test.ts (new @mutate on the Not Now line). Was: pushPermissionNudge.ts treats dismissed dialog as browser-denied, misinforming any user who ever blocked notifications — 'Not Now' on the push prompt incorrectly claims notifications are off. (archive L6722)

- [x] **Q915 Stale remote branches pile up: prune them every 6h, safely (2026-10-01).** delete_branch_on_merge only fires on a MERGED PR, so branches of PRs closed unmerged and stray pushes stayed forever. FIXED: scripts/prune-stale-branches.mjs, run by .github/workflows/branch-prune.yml (every 6h + push to main), deletes a remote branch only when `git cherry origin/main` shows every patch on main, no open PR uses it, it is not main or land/*, and its tip is at least 24h old; each delete is leased on the measured tip (--force-with-lease) so a branch pushed to since survives. Branches with patches not on main are listed UNLANDED in the job summary, never deleted. GUARD: src/test/pruneStaleBranches.test.ts (4 @mutate lines: cherry sign, land/* protection, 24h age, lease). Red runs reach main-red-watch.

## Archived 2026-10-02 — from "MORNING QUESTIONS (held overnight 2026-09-23 while the owner sleeps)"

- [x] **Q57 Nightly refresh jobs can PROVE a file is stale but can't UPDATE it.** STATUS 2026-09-23: OWNER SETTING DONE (verified via API: default_workflow_permissions=write, can_approve_pull_request_reviews=true). WIRED (branch cloud/q57-refresh-prs): shared composite .github/actions/refresh-pr rebuilds ONE bot branch per refresh (bot/refresh/<id>) from latest main, commits only its declared paths, skips when nothing changed (and closes a leftover PR), else opens or updates ONE PR and runs `gh pr merge --auto --squash`; never pushes main. Used by a `land` job (the workflow's only contents/pull-requests write) in scoreboard.yml (SCOREBOARD.md + the OPEN.md Everything-open block, rebuilt on latest main via `scoreboard.mjs --live-from`), loading-states-refresh.yml (measurements.json), write-contract-refresh.yml (snapshot; drift now lands instead of failing, a reject still fails), staleness-watch.yml (schedule/dispatch: `npm run inventories:refresh` on latest main) and morning-page.yml (docs/morning/, Q67). Not landed, with reasons in the guard: ui-sweep.yml (overlay baseline is a findings ratchet), vacuity.yml (same report staleness-watch lands), db-drift-detect.yml (types drift needs code fixes). GUARD: src/test/refreshWorkflowsOpenPrs.test.ts (set derived from scheduled workflows running a generator registered in scripts/check-generated-current.mjs, two-way; red on main before wiring: 3 of 6 failed, 4 workflows unwired; 5 @mutate, each killed). OWNER/LEAD WATCH: (1) with github.token a PR triggers no pull_request workflows, so the step dispatches `vars.REFRESH_PR_CHECK_WORKFLOWS` (default test.yml vitest.yml) on the bot branch; those must cover main's required checks, or add a `REFRESH_PR_TOKEN` secret (PAT/app token) so PRs trigger normally; (2) auto-merge needs Settings -> General -> Allow auto-merge (the land job fails loudly if not); (3) a merge made with github.token triggers no push workflows on main. TICK [x] when the first bot PR auto-merges. BLOCKED ON: no bot PR has merged BY auto-merge yet. MEASURED 2026-09-28 (gh pr list, head bot/refresh/*): 4 merged (#1722, #1774 on 09-26 04:23Z; #1862, #1864 on 09-26 19:49Z), each with auto-merge enabled by app/github-actions but mergedBy louisianahelpr in pairs seconds apart (a by-hand merge, not auto-merge); #1932, #1943, #1952 OPEN.  Why the open ones have not auto-merged was not investigated. **DONE 2026-10-02 (lane pd-b), MEASURED (GitHub PR API):** 0 bot refresh PRs auto-merged as of 2026-09-27 -> #1943 (scoreboard) merged 2026-10-01 01:08:58Z by app/github-actions with auto-merge, then #1923 (02:14Z) and #1952 (04:56Z); later #2029 and #2038 also auto-merged.

- [x] **Q572** (LOW, found working Q349, 2026-09-26, measured once): listing a Storage bucket as anon fails with an ERROR instead of a clean empty/denied answer. `POST /storage/v1/object/list/avatars` with the publishable key returned 403 `{"code":"AccessDenied","message":"permission denied for table jobs"}`, so a storage.objects SELECT policy reads public.jobs, which anon has no grant on. The refusal itself is correct (anon should not list avatars); the problem is that it comes from a permission error inside a policy rather than the policy's own condition, so any anon storage read that reaches that policy errors. Not read: pg_policies for storage.objects (no service role here). To close: find the policy with `select policyname, qual from pg_policies where schemaname='storage' and tablename='objects' and qual ilike '%jobs%'`, and scope it `TO authenticated` (or guard the jobs read) so anon gets a plain deny; add it to the anon-grant class guard. **TICKED 2026-10-03 (lead, verified on prod):** e952452d7 re-scoped the storage.objects policies that read public.jobs TO authenticated (migration 20261002051450); pg_policies on prod: all six such policies are {authenticated}, so anon gets a plain deny. Guard: src/test/storagePoliciesAreAuthenticated.test.ts.

- [x] **Q909 MEDIUM Identity verification no longer gates posting or hiring (owner 2026-10-01), but identity still feeds the credential tier and nothing can start IDV any more.** Done 2026-10-01 (migration 20261001222911_remove_idv_requirement.sql; guard src/test/identityNeverGatesPostOrAward.test.ts): the jobs INSERT policy and helper_award_block_reason no longer consult idv_status; the Profile "Verify your ID" banner, IDVPromptDialog, VerificationStatusRow and verificationPrompt.ts are deleted. LEFT, needs an owner call: (1) get_user_credential_tier still counts identity, and Post Job still offers a minimum tier "1 = ID-verified" (usePostJobForm), yet no UI starts Stripe Identity now, so a new account can never reach tier 1 and a poster who sets it shuts out every new Helpr. - [x] **Q721 MEDIUM OWNER DECISION: stale-deploy WebKit red (#1863) vs the Q178 first-paint budget** (measured 2026-09-26, local `vite preview` + `happy-path-webkit`). The 12 red tests in stale-deploy.spec.ts ("warm, guard armed … Try Again recovers" ×10, boot "stale HTML" ×2) all fail the same way: when a chunk fetch fails, WebKit caches the failure, and the recovery reload (`hardReloadBypassCache`, `_v=` URL) re-imports the same chunk URL from that cache without touching the network, so the page stays blank. The failure comes from Q178's `modulePreload.resolveDependencies` returning the deps list, which includes the chunk itself. Four variants measured: **DONE 2026-10-03 (verify pass, lead-confirmed):** 6db01bf88 (on main) refetches the failed chunk with cache reload in WebKit without touching the first-paint budget; nightly-webkit run 37129193690 (ca5acf08d) green including the stale-deploy tests; issue #1863 closed by github-actions[bot] (lead-checked). GUARD: e2e/happy-path/stale-deploy.spec.ts + src/lib/chunkReload.test.ts.
  - `() => []`: **34/34 WebKit pass**, but `critical-path.mjs --check` gives 11 failures. The page chunk moves from round 2 to round 3, and readiness takes 7-8 rounds instead of 2, on /, /browse, /login, /signup and /posts.
  - `deps.filter(d => d !== filename)`: 10/10 fail.
  - Removing the `<link rel=modulepreload>` tags before the reload: 10/10 fail.
  - Keeping deps only when the importer is the entry or main chunk: 12 fail AND the perf budget still fails.

- [x] **Q713 HIGH (renumbered from Q411 on landing; was Q411 in its PR) A job status change fails outright when a blocked user is in the job's message thread (pre-existing; found by the lh-authz-rls review of PR #1820, 2026-09-26).** insert_job_status_system_message (AFTER UPDATE OF status ON jobs, SECURITY DEFINER) inserts a system message from the poster to every thread participant; enforce_block_on_message_insert raises "You can't message this user." when the poster and that participant are blocked, which aborts the whole status change (accept / start / complete / cancel / dispute) for whoever made it. Measured 0 live instances (thread participants x user_blocks x owned jobs = 0 rows, 2026-09-26). Fix direction: skip blocked participants in the system-message insert (NOT by exempting is_system rows from the block trigger: a client could set is_system on INSERT until Q340, so that would let a message cross a block) — messaging-lane objects, so not changed in PR #1820. Guard to name when fixed: a PGlite probe where a status change on a job whose thread holds a blocked participant succeeds and sends that participant no message. FIXED 2026-10-03 (lane B), live after db-deploy: migration 20261003183349_status_message_skips_blocked_participants restates insert_job_status_system_message from its live body (last defined by 20260924013306) plus one predicate, `public.are_users_blocked(NEW.customer_id, p.participant) IS NOT TRUE`, the block trigger's own test, so a participant blocked with the poster (either direction) is skipped and gets no message, and the status change goes through; the block trigger is unchanged and still refuses a person messaging someone blocked. Live 2026-10-03: 3 blocks, 0 inside a job thread. PROOF: src/test/pglite/statusMessageSkipsBlocked.pglite.mjs (live bodies of is_server_context, are_users_blocked, enforce_block_on_message_insert, insert_job_status_system_message; ALL PASS applied 3x; NEW_MIGRATION=skip 4 FAILED: the poster's completion, the other block direction and the Helpr's move all abort with "You can't message this user.", and the service role's change messages the blocked participant). GUARD: src/test/serverMessageInsertsSkipBlocked.test.ts (every function the migrations leave that INSERTs INTO messages, with or without a column list, screens its recipients with are_users_blocked; red on the tree without the migration) and the exact are_users_blocked caller inventory in src/test/areUsersBlockedPartyOnly.test.ts (Q348: the new caller is a trigger, so it gets the real answer through pg_trigger_depth() > 0). REVIEW lh-authz-rls 2026-10-03: APPROVE (body = live md5 f6af2fb561e8281dfc6795ef2fb2ae2d plus the predicate; after deploy md5(prosrc) must read d2e0b47244387473bd586cb602e20841; the block can no longer leak through the 42501 to whoever moved the status); its LOW/NIT notes folded in. The same abort can still come from the message rate limit: Q1169. done-when: sql `SELECT bool_and(position('are_users_blocked' in p.prosrc) > 0 AND position('p.participant) IS NOT TRUE' in p.prosrc) > 0)::text FROM pg_proc p WHERE p.proname = 'insert_job_status_system_message' AND p.pronamespace = 'public'::regnamespace` => true **DONE 2026-10-03 (lead, verified live after #2212):** prod's insert_job_status_system_message has md5(prosrc) d2e0b47244387473bd586cb602e20841, schema_migrations has 20261003183349, and the done-when query above reads true (read-only SQL, 21:3x Z).
- [x] **Q1176 MEDIUM A live read fails the whole check on a transient CLI login failure (db-drift-detect red 2026-10-03 17:17Z, #2205).** function-body-drift.mjs's `supabase db query --linked` got "failed to connect as temp role ... password authentication failed for user cli_login_postgres (SQLSTATE 28P01)"; the same job's other live reads passed, the re-run went green and #2205 closed itself. Eleven scripts call the CLI directly (function-body-drift and write-contract even in CI, where the Management API would avoid the temp role). Fix at the source: one helper with a bounded retry on the transient signatures (and the Management API when its env is set), used by all eleven; guard: no script calls `supabase db query` except through it. **DONE 2026-10-03 (lead):** scripts/lib/supabaseDbQuery.mjs runs `supabase db query` with up to 3 tries, 5 s apart, ONLY for the temp-role login / dropped-connection signatures (anything else throws at once, unchanged, so a broken check still fails the run); the ten scripts that called the CLI directly go through it; db-deploy.yml re-runs when it changes; liveCheckScriptsFailClosed recognises it as a live read. The blip cannot be produced on demand, so the retry is proven on the CLI's exact 2026-10-03 error text through a stubbed runner, each signature on its own. GUARD: src/test/liveQueriesRetryTransientLogin.test.ts (no script runs `db query` except through the helper: red on the ten direct calls; 3 @mutate, each killed by vacuity --only).

- [x] **Q1180 HIGH (renumbered from Q1169 on landing: main took Q1169 for the message rate limit) Hire sends the offer; the Helpr finishes payout setup and Stripe ID right after tapping Accept, and only then is the acceptance complete (owner 2026-10-02 and 2026-10-03, stated over several messages).** Spec, in the owner's words where given: the poster's Hire is never blocked by the Helpr's Stripe state; when the Helpr taps Accept, "thank you for accepting the offer, in order to fully accept these 2 things must be done before we will notify the poster that you have accepted" (payout setup and Stripe ID); "it's required immediately after they accept"; "if they have any of it done already there is no need to require it again" (ask only for what is missing; both done = the accept completes at once); "it shouldn't hold up anything" (no strike for an offer that lapses while setup is unfinished). Nothing starts, finishes or is disputed on an offer that was not accepted (accept_required, ready or not); no strike while setup is unfinished (expiry and decline); a Helpr who never accepted is no no-show; decline refuses anything but a live offer; only an accepted Helpr closes the other applications. WHEN: AFTER the job's terminal flip, never before (the review's HIGH: a restore before the flip, with the Quick Refund claim handed back, let a withdrawn dispute walk the still-disputed job to a payout on top of the refund). create-payment's new returnGiftAfterRefund runs after a successful flip (Quick Refund still holds its claim; a general refund whose flip missed restores nothing), reads the payout ledger first and restores nothing when a transfer (pending/paid/reversed) moved money out of the escrow (a payout batch already in flight can still land after the flip: Q1211), never throws, pages critical on a refusal or failure and answers giftRestoreFailed: true beside success, counts the gift in the dispute record and the audit rows, and tells the poster only when THIS call minted a gift that can be spent (a revoked donation's replacement cannot). charge.refunded restores after its flip, before the ledger row and the notice, only when no decided dispute owns the escrow AND no payout transfer (pending/paid/reversed) moved out of it (else a critical page, no throw); a failed restore with a gift at stake throws so Stripe redelivers. ## Archived 2026-10-04 — from "QUEUE (cont.) — carried; the retired audit-bus section's table (2026-09-23) is superseded by the FEEDS mirror above"

- [x] **Q550 DONE 2026-10-04 (branch cloud/ci-guards): a11y-webkit-prod.yml's sweep holds the shared-accounts lock.** The two-engine matrix is now ONE `sweep` job (chromium, then webkit, `--output test-results/<engine>` so neither empties the other's results) with `concurrency: prod-lifecycle-shared-accounts`, cancel-in-progress false, `needs: [preflight, wait-accounts, fixtures]` (the Q743 queue) and `timeout-minutes: 60`; artifact names and the diff job's paths are unchanged. The dispatch was already out of prod-load and DISPATCH_SHARES_GROUP is empty, so that part of the old line was done. GUARD: src/test/sharedAccountJobsHoldOneLock.test.ts (NOT_LOCKED entry deleted; `a11y-webkit-prod.yml:sweep` now in the inventory floor; new rule: no locked job is a parallel matrix, so the shape cannot return; 24 tests, 3 new @mutate killed). RED on main's workflow files: 9 of 33 tests across the two guards fail. NOT measured: a real run of the new job (timing: chromium 9.0 + webkit 16.1 min measured in run 36148473443, now sequential, against the 60-minute cap). Was: **Q550 MEDIUM** (found by Q326, 2026-09-26): a11y-webkit-prod.yml's sweep job signs in as the shared test accounts but does not hold the `prod-lifecycle-shared-accounts` job lock. Its two-engine matrix (chromium + webkit side by side for the diff) is why: one lock would serialise the engines, and GitHub's one-pending-per-group rule could cancel the queued engine. Its workflow group is prod-load on every trigger, so it never overlaps a scheduled suite or a press run, but a DISPATCHED split suite (journeys, prod-audit, slow-network, loading-states, privacy) can still overlap it. Fix: run both engines inside one job that holds the lock, as press-every-control now does (scripts/audit/press-wave.sh), then take its dispatch out of prod-load and remove it from DISPATCH_SHARES_GROUP. Guard to update: src/test/sharedAccountJobsHoldOneLock.test.ts (delete its NOT_LOCKED entry; the test fails until you do).
- [x] **Q551 DONE 2026-10-04 (branch cloud/ci-guards): vacuity.yml no longer drives the shared accounts outside the lock.** Option 1 of the old line: the mutation phase is split by kind (scripts/vacuity/run.mjs `kindOf`: a guard under e2e/ is e2e). `vacuity` runs `--no-e2e` and holds NO shared-account secret; `vacuity-e2e` runs `--e2e` with the poster/helper secrets, holds `prod-lifecycle-shared-accounts` behind the Q743 waiter, and exists only when the lock-free `scope` job (scripts/vacuity/scope.mjs, same scoping function as the gate) selects an e2e registration, so a push that changed none never queues behind a press run. The waiter now counts vacuity.yml PUSH runs (PUSH_DRIVERS) so their pending lock job is not the one a third arrival cancels. GUARD: src/test/vacuityE2eIsLocked.test.ts (11 tests, 10 @mutate killed) plus src/test/sharedAccountJobsHoldOneLock.test.ts (NOT_LOCKED entry deleted) and sharedAccountLockQueues.test.ts. RED on main's vacuity.yml: 9 of 33 tests fail. Measured scope on this branch: e2e=0 unit=1073 for a push; e2e=96 of 4092 for the full set. Follow-ups filed: Q1208, Q1209. NOT verified: a real CI run of the split (scope output, waiter on a push run, `needs.vacuity-e2e` in the nightly status). Was: **Q551 MEDIUM** (found by Q326, 2026-09-26): vacuity.yml's vacuity job holds shared-account credentials without the account lock. It runs on every push, and in the lock each push's guard proof would queue behind a 5-hour press run and be cancelled by the next push. It signs in only when a registered e2e guard's files changed, but then it drives the shared accounts beside whatever else is running. Fix options: move the credentialed (e2e) registrations into a separate job that holds the lock and runs only when such a registration is selected; or make those registrations use a dedicated seed account no other suite drives. Guard to update: src/test/sharedAccountJobsHoldOneLock.test.ts (its NOT_LOCKED entry).

- [x] **Q1169 HIGH The message rate limit can abort a job status change, the way a block did (found by the lh-authz-rls review of Q713, 2026-10-03; pre-existing; reproduced in PGlite on the live bodies, not on prod).** enforce_message_rate (BEFORE INSERT ON messages) has no exemption for a server-side or system row: it counts the sender's rows in the last hour (system rows included, and rows inserted earlier in the same statement are visible) against a cap of 30. insert_job_status_system_message posts its notice from the poster to every thread participant inside the status UPDATE, so: a poster who has messaged 31 distinct people on a job can never have its status changed again (the Helpr's start and the poster's own cancel both abort with "You are sending messages too quickly. Please slow down."); with 30 messages in the last hour the Helpr's completion aborts; at 29 the status notice itself writes a message_flooding fraud flag against the poster. Fix direction (after Q340 is live, which stops clients setting is_system/created_at on INSERT): `IF pg_trigger_depth() > 1 THEN RETURN NEW; END IF;` in enforce_message_rate and count only `NOT is_system` rows. GUARD when built: a PGlite probe (a poster at the cap: the status change succeeds, every participant gets the notice, no fraud flag) and an extension of src/test/serverMessageInsertsSkipBlocked.test.ts to every BEFORE INSERT trigger on messages that can RAISE. **FIXED 2026-10-03 (branch lead/messages-guards, not landed; live after db-deploy):** migration 20261004001242_messages_status_notices_and_sender_writes restates enforce_message_rate from its live body (md5 aa9fc92254824c20cb1a434624bbf0c5, last defined by 20260907231710) plus `IF NEW.is_system AND pg_trigger_depth() > 1 THEN RETURN NEW; END IF;` (both halves required, not the depth alone) and `AND NOT is_system` in its count, and restates can_send_message_to_in_job (the INSERT policy's own copy of the count, live md5 adde58ba323ddb81f0cb78d59972c7d7) with `AND NOT m.is_system`: without that copy, after a big fan-out the poster still could not send anyone a message for an hour (RLS refused first). Hole check, live 2026-10-03: Q340 is live (authenticated has no INSERT on is_system, created_at, read, read_at, edited_at or id: has_column_privilege false for each); the only function in any schema that INSERTs INTO messages is insert_job_status_system_message, fired only by jobs AFTER UPDATE OF status; no view, rule or INSTEAD OF trigger writes messages; edge functions only read messages. A notice-shaped row from an RPC (depth 1) and an ordinary row relayed by another trigger are still counted and refused at the cap (proof L5, L6). With notices off the send limit nothing else bounded them (lh-authz-rls review of 6807d082a, CHANGES REQUIRED, read live here): rpc_open_dispute and rpc_withdraw_dispute are EXECUTE-able by authenticated and flip a job in_progress <-> disputed at will, every move notifies every participant, each notice writes a type 'message' notification that suppress_exact_duplicate_notification exempts, and fan_out_push_on_notification pushes it unthrottled. So the same migration restates insert_job_status_system_message (live md5 d2e0b47244387473bd586cb602e20841) with a brake at the source, per participant: skip when (a) their LATEST notice on this job already says the same thing, or (b) they got 6 notices on this job in the last 10 minutes; the status change itself always goes through. A real move back (X -> Y -> X) is still announced: the first brake (24f9ec692, the same text anywhere in 10 minutes) dropped it, which the lh-authz-rls re-review reproduced (in_progress -> disputed -> withdrawn left the thread at "Dispute opened"). Cost: the same notice twice in a row with nothing between (completed, revision, completed) is said once, and an alternating loop stops being announced after 6 in 10 minutes. Two moves inside one transaction share now(), so (a) cannot order them (not measured whether any code path does that). After deploy md5(prosrc) must read db66ded9018c6ca4af79647c2d632d3e (enforce_message_rate), 9fbc0f481c21c33564886b0aef89f304 (can_send_message_to_in_job) and 7b9df9c20d46f0dadc1e6489c55b0c14 (insert_job_status_system_message). PROOF: src/test/pglite/messageRateSparesStatusNotices.pglite.mjs on src/test/pglite/messagesWorld.mjs (prod's messages table, grants, 7 policies, 13 of its 16 triggers plus jobs' status trigger, and 20 function bodies each md5-pinned to prod) ALL PASS applied 3x (26 checks); NEW_MIGRATION=skip 9 FAILED (a 32-participant job's start, cancel and service-role completion abort; the completion of a poster at 30 aborts; the notice writes the flag at 29; after 31 notices the poster's own message and the wrapper are refused; the dispute loop aborts at round 6; a back-to-back repeat is sent twice). R8, 16 in_progress -> disputed -> in_progress rounds with 3 participants: 96 notices and 96 message notifications on 6807d082a (red), 18 and 18 (the cap) now; L8 and L9, X -> Y -> X (in_progress -> disputed -> in_progress with "Work started" 5 minutes earlier; revision_requested -> disputed -> completed with "Job completed" 5 minutes earlier) end the thread at the job's real state: red on 24f9ec692's migration (run with MIGRATION_SQL_FILE), green now; L10: awarded, started, completed in quick succession are all sent; L11: the cap is a window. GUARD: src/test/serverMessageInsertsSkipBlocked.test.ts now covers every INSERT trigger on messages that can RAISE (inventory from the migrations through src/test/helpers/migrationTriggers.ts, which reads the three format()-loop attaches too: 16 triggers, prod's 16 by name; 5 raise: block, rate, reply check, ban gate, unconfirmed-email gate), each cleared two-way against the code (the rate one needs its exemption before any RAISE and every server insert to be a trigger writing is_system = true), and each server notice insert must carry both halves of the brake (a latest-notice check per recipient, and a per-recipient count in a window capped at no more than 10), plus the parity of the trigger's and the policy's counts (5 @mutate lines; the Q713 one re-pointed at this migration). Review: lh-authz-rls on 6807d082a: CHANGES REQUIRED (must-fix: the unbounded notice loop; Q1166 and Q1167 approvable as written), addressed by the repeat brake above; re-review of 24f9ec692: CHANGES REQUIRED (the brake dropped X -> Y -> X), addressed by the latest-notice check and cap above; re-review pending (lead). The loop's effects outside the thread are Q1223. done-when: sql `SELECT (position('NEW.is_system AND pg_trigger_depth() > 1' in r.prosrc) > 0 AND position('AND NOT is_system' in r.prosrc) > 0 AND position('AND NOT m.is_system' in w.prosrc) > 0 AND position('d.created_at > now() - interval ''10 minutes''' in n.prosrc) > 0 AND position('WHERE last.content = v_content' in n.prosrc) > 0)::text FROM pg_proc r, pg_proc w, pg_proc n WHERE r.proname = 'enforce_message_rate' AND r.pronamespace = 'public'::regnamespace AND w.proname = 'can_send_message_to_in_job' AND w.pronamespace = 'public'::regnamespace AND n.proname = 'insert_job_status_system_message' AND n.pronamespace = 'public'::regnamespace` => true **Round 3 (lh-authz-rls, 2026-10-04): APPROVE with notes**; built by the lead: the cap never holds back a final state (cancelled, completed; PGlite L12 + control), R9 walks a transition prod allows (in_progress -> revision_requested -> in_progress), and the latest-notice order is documented (every status RPC locks the job FOR UPDATE first). insert_job_status_system_message md5 is now 7b9df9c20d46f0dadc1e6489c55b0c14. **DONE 2026-10-04 (lead, verified live):** db-deploy of 1e45f3b8d succeeded and prod schema_migrations has 20261004001242, 20261004001807, 20261004162818, 20261004162921 and 20261004165404; the done-when above, run read-only on prod, reads as expected: enforce_message_rate, can_send_message_to_in_job and insert_job_status_system_message carry the round-3 bodies (done-when true).

Only a stale or hostile client does this. Stamp writers, inventoried from the effective definitions, the edge functions and the app: complete_job_accept (flag), claim_series_dates and respond_to_direct_offer (take-an-open-job), charge-recurring-visits (edge, service role), app none; the harnesses that PATCHed it (e2e/prod-audit/fundedOpenJob.ts, scripts/ci/race-runner.mjs race 2, scripts/probes/recurring-helper-patch.prod.mjs) now accept through accept_job_offer or set the flag. Follow-ups: Q1194, Q1195, Q1196. - [x] **Q1188 LOW accept_job_offer's profile-then-job lock can deadlock with the strike ladder's job-then-profile writes (lh-authz-rls round 3 of Q1180, should-fix 2).** A cycle needs the same Helpr on the same job at the same moment; Postgres aborts one side. If that is the hourly expire_unanswered_offers sweep, the whole sweep rolls back and retries next hour. Mitigation: a per-iteration BEGIN/EXCEPTION in expire_unanswered_offers, logging the job. **STATUS 2026-10-03 (branch lead/accept-gate-hardening): fixed, review pending, not landed.** 20261004001807 expires each offer in its own subtransaction: a failure (a deadlock or anything else) rolls back that offer alone, is logged to error_logs with its job, Helpr and SQLSTATE (a seed job or seed Helpr as info under 'expire_unanswered_offers-seed', which error_log_is_seed() keeps out of Slack), and the sweep goes on; the next hourly run retries that offer. PROOF: src/test/pglite/acceptGateHardening.pglite.mjs B1-B3: on prod's body one 40P01 raised inside the strike rolled back the other offer's expiry too; with the migration only that offer stays, logged. PGlite has one connection, so the deadlock is raised there, not reproduced between two sessions. GUARD: src/test/acceptGateHardening.test.ts (the Q1188 block). Left: review, land, live. done-when: sql `SELECT (position('unanswered offer expiry failed' in (SELECT prosrc FROM pg_proc WHERE proname = 'expire_unanswered_offers' AND pronamespace = 'public'::regnamespace)) > 0)::text` => true **DONE 2026-10-04 (lead, verified live):** db-deploy of 1e45f3b8d succeeded and prod schema_migrations has 20261004001242, 20261004001807, 20261004162818, 20261004162921 and 20261004165404; the done-when above, run read-only on prod, reads as expected: expire_unanswered_offers logs a stuck offer instead of rolling back (done-when true).
- [x] **Q1189 LOW jobs.created_at is client-writable, so the storage sweep's 90-day trust window and the browse freshness score can be forged (lh-authz-rls review of Q1181, 2026-10-03, measured on prod).** The poster can write jobs.created_at (and id) on insert: enforce_jobs_insert_column_lock does not reset it, neither enforce_poster_jobs_money_lock nor prevent_job_field_escalation lists it, and no CHECK bounds it; seed status is self-serve (is_fixture_email matches any @mailinator.com signup). After a non-seed log row is pruned at 90 days, a mailinator account could insert a job with that id and an old created_at, delete it, and have its leftover files swept with no cap (bounded: only files orphanReason already calls job-gone). get_ranked_open_jobs' freshness and the early-access cutoff read the same column. Fix: NEW.created_at := now() for client inserts in the insert lock, and created_at in locked_always. Guard: a PGlite case, red first. **STATUS 2026-10-04 (cloud/booked-job-lock): fixed in 20261004165404_booked_job_place_and_details_locked.sql.** enforce_jobs_insert_column_lock (restated from its effective 20260924044812 text) sets NEW.created_at := now() for a signed-in poster's INSERT, and created_at joins locked_always on UPDATE; server contexts (service role seeds, charge-recurring-visits) pass through. PROOF: src/test/pglite/bookedJobLocked.pglite.mjs (C1-C6; RED on main: C1 a backdated insert keeps 2020, C4/C5 a PATCH of created_at lands; ALL PASS after, applied 3x). GUARD: src/test/bookedJobPlaceLocked.test.ts ("jobs.created_at is server-owned", with @mutate). Not fixed: jobs.id is still client-chosen on insert, filed as Q1195. Needs an lh-authz-rls review. done-when: sql `SELECT ((SELECT prosrc FROM pg_proc WHERE proname = 'enforce_jobs_insert_column_lock' AND pronamespace = 'public'::regnamespace) LIKE '%NEW.created_at %:= now()%' AND (SELECT prosrc FROM pg_proc WHERE proname = 'enforce_poster_jobs_money_lock' AND pronamespace = 'public'::regnamespace) LIKE '%''created_at''%')::text` => true **DONE 2026-10-04 (lead, verified live):** db-deploy of 1e45f3b8d succeeded and prod schema_migrations has 20261004001242, 20261004001807, 20261004162818, 20261004162921 and 20261004165404; the done-when above, run read-only on prod, reads as expected: jobs.created_at is server-owned on insert and locked for the poster (done-when true).

Android ships neither Georgia nor Liberation Serif, so both "Bodoni Moda Fallback" families resolve to nothing and the pre-font frame uses the system serif (Noto Serif). To close: measure CLS on a real Android Chrome with Bodoni throttled; if it is over 0.02, add a "Bodoni Moda Fallback Noto" family (local("Noto Serif"), metrics by the method in src/index.css's fallback comment, measured against NotoSerif-{Regular,Bold,BoldItalic}) after the Liberation one, and extend src/test/heroFontSwapDoesNotShift.test.ts to require it. **CLOSED 2026-10-04 (owner, pop-up): obsolete: the owner chose iOS only (2026-10-03); there is no Android app.** NO-GUARD: not a defect.

- [x] **Q447 MEDIUM** (OWNER DECISION, OA-018 follow-ups, 2026-09-25, from GoTrue's source, not reproduced on a device): (a) PRE-ACCOUNT DATA, **MEDIUM** (rated by the lh-authz-rls review of #1806, 2026-09-26): when an UNverified email account is linked (Q446 step 4), GoTrue removes its password and email identity to stop a pre-account takeover, but OUR profile row (name, phone, DOB, photo, city, bio, marketing consent, legal acceptances, referral) is kept as whoever typed it at signup. Wider than first listed (code read 2026-09-26): complete-signup's unauthenticated path (supabase/functions/complete-signup/index.ts ~200-269: account under 30 min old, never signed in, empty bio) also takes the avatar, license and insurance uploads, writes legal_acceptances and records the referral, and because it fills every ProtectedRoute gate field the victim never passes /complete-profile to see it; the attacker's phone becomes the contact shown to counterparties. GoTrue leaves the attacker no session, refresh token, MFA factor or phone identity (token.go refuses the password grant to an unconfirmed email; signup refuses email+phone together), and cleanup-abandoned-accounts deletes unconfirmed accounts after 30 days. Referral attribution should also require a confirmed email. If an attacker signed up first with a victim's address, the victim's Google sign-in lands in an account whose profile the attacker wrote. Options: keep (today), or clear the signup-entered profile fields when email_confirmed_at is first set by a provider link (no email identity left), sending the person back through /complete-profile. Clearing also costs a legitimate user who signed up and then used Google instead of the email link. (b) APPLE HIDE-MY-EMAIL makes a second account for someone who already has one; /complete-profile could say "Already have a Helpr account under another email? Sign out and log in with it" when the address is @privaterelay.appleid.com. Guard to name when done: a case in scripts/sql/identity-linking-scenarios.sql for (a); a /complete-profile test for (b). **OWNER DECIDED 2026-10-04 (pop-up): when the real email owner takes over a never-verified account, everything typed before verification is DELETED (profile fields, uploads and their storage objects, consents, legal acceptances, referral); the owner fills in their own profile.** **STATUS 2026-10-04 (security & data lane): (a) built per the owner's decision of 2026-10-04 (DELETE everything typed before verification), not landed; needs an lh-authz-rls review. Storage rows cannot be deleted from SQL (storage.protect_delete), so cleanup-abandoned-accounts drains them through the Storage API (supabase/functions/_shared/preVerificationWipeSweep.ts), only objects unchanged since the wipe (a new avatar under the same key is never touched); a failed removal is a cron defect. After the wipe the ProtectedRoute gate fields are empty, so the real owner lands on /complete-profile. - [x] **Q1209 LOW The admin refunds do not check what the charge already refunded before refunding again (found by the lh-money-escrow review of Q454, 2026-10-03; code read only).** **STATUS 2026-10-04 (money lane): FIXED in code (rebased on #2252, which carried Q1192/Q1193).** create-payment gains existingRefundCovering(): when the charge's own refunds already cover what this call would refund, the existing live (succeeded or pending) refund is reused and recorded (payment_refunds upsert on stripe_refund_id), and stripe.refunds.create is not called. admin_refund_dispute reads latest_charge.amount_refunded (the PaymentIntent is already expanded) against its refund amount; admin_refund_general (FULL only) sums the refunds it already lists for the sequence key against the capture; a partial never reuses. RED before: src/test/edge/create-payment.test.ts 'Q1209 ...' dispute and general cases (500: a second refunds.create); GREEN after (create-payment 170/170, 31 guards that read create-payment 420/420), plus a control that a partial prior refund still refunds the rest; @mutate on both reuse lines. Needs an lh-money-escrow review; redeploys on merge. done-when: test src/test/edge/create-payment.test.ts create-payment admin_refund_dispute and admin_refund_general call stripe.refunds.create with a fixed idempotency key (refund-dispute-<job>, refund-general-<job>-full) and never read the charge's amount_refunded first, as cancel_escrow does (it skips the call when latest_charge.amount_refunded already covers it). Inside Stripe's ~24h key window a retry replays the same refund; after it, a retry is refused by Stripe (the charge is already refunded), so a job whose flip failed after a refund can never be finished from the button (it pages and needs a hand). A double refund would need a capture of about 63 cents or less, below the $10 job minimum. Fix: read latest_charge.amount_refunded and reuse the existing refund (as cancel_escrow does). Guard to add: an edge case per action in src/test/edge/create-payment.test.ts. **DONE 2026-10-04 (auto-tick, verified live): every done-when marker on this item read as expected on prod (src/test/edge/create-payment.test.ts passes).**

FIX in both: a gift job that also carries a PaymentIntent or session reads it; a captured shortfall counts toward the cap (card + gift, as execute-dispute-split does) but is never the transfer's source_transaction (it holds only the shortfall); a card leg with no intent, a test-mode one, or one that never captured adds nothing (the old cap); an unreadable one defers (release-payout 502, process-scheduled-payouts verify_error). RED before the fix: the 'pays it' and 'defers' cases in src/test/edge/release-payout.test.ts and src/test/edge/process-scheduled-payouts.test.ts (Q1210 blocks) failed with 409 / exceeds_captured_escrow; GREEN after; @mutate on each new branch. Needs an lh-money-escrow review before landing. Both functions redeploy on merge (functions-deploy.yml). REVIEW (lh-money-escrow 2026-10-04, APPROVE with notes): (a) a mixed job's transfer now has no source_transaction, so it draws on the available platform balance and can fail with balance_insufficient; that goes through the existing failed-transfer retry path (failClaim, salted key), not a new one. (b) the PaymentIntent's amount_received is not reduced by refunds, so a partly refunded shortfall still counts in full toward the cap (the same figure the non-gift path and execute-dispute-split use). Both left as they are. done-when: test src/test/edge/release-payout.test.ts done-when: test src/test/edge/process-scheduled-payouts.test.ts release-payout reads the PaymentIntent only when the job has NO redeemed gift (index.ts:536, `if (!giftCardRow)`), so on a mixed job escrowAmountReceivedCents stays null and the payout cap (escrowValueCents, :808) is the gift's applied value alone; a Helpr payout figured from the whole budget is then refused as 'exceeds captured escrow' whenever the card part is larger than the fee margin. The reviewer read the same shape in process-scheduled-payouts. Verify with an edge case (gift + PaymentIntent, both legs) before fixing; the fix is to read the PaymentIntent for a mixed job too and cap at captured + gift, as execute-dispute-split does. **DONE 2026-10-04 (auto-tick, verified live): every done-when marker on this item read as expected on prod (src/test/edge/release-payout.test.ts passes; src/test/edge/process-scheduled-payouts.test.ts passes).**

AFTER its claim row exists it re-reads the job: still payout_pending = proceed; moved = the unsent claim is set 'canceled' and the call is blocked (process-scheduled-payouts reports job_moved_on, no defect; release-payout 409); unreadable = error, claim released; an ORPHANED claim on a moved job is never resumed (error: its transfer may exist). Q454's gift restore reads the payout ledger after its flip, so it no longer adds a returned gift on top, but the card-plus-payout double spend stands. - [x] **Q1222 MEDIUM A tip Checkout opened before a payout hold and paid after it still pays the held Helpr (lh-money-escrow review of Q764, 2026-10-04; code read only).** **STATUS 2026-10-04 later (money lane, lead's design call: a sweep in process-scheduled-payouts, no admin edge action, Q781 stands): BUILT, deploy pending; supersedes the STATUS below.** checkout.session.completed now runs holdBackPaidTip (_shared/heldTipRepay.ts) on every paid tip: if the Helpr is on a payout hold, a public.tip_hold_redrives claim row records the debt ('owed') and the tip's transfer is REVERSED back to the platform under key tip-hold-reverse-<tip> ('reversed'); the poster is never refunded (tipsAreFinal stays green); the Helpr is not told yet. process-scheduled-payouts runs redriveHeldTips every run: once the hold is released it claims the row ('reversed' -> 'repaying', compare-and-set), adopts a re-pay Stripe already holds (its own tip_<tipId> transfer group, metadata tip_id) or creates one under key tip-hold-repay-<tip>, marks 'repaid' and tells the Helpr; a stale 'repaying' row resumes under the same key; a stale 'owed' row is reversed again (same key) or marked 'kept' if the hold is already gone; a refused reversal is 'not_reversed' + critical page; a refused re-pay is 'failed' + critical page. Migration 20261004220059_tip_held_payout_redrive.sql (server-only table, RLS on, no client grants). PGlite proof src/test/pglite/tipHoldRedrive.pglite.mjs: OLD STATE RED (no table), NEW GREEN applied 3x (one claim row per tip, closed status set, CAS claim matches once, unique re-pay transfer, no client privileges). Unreachable live today (payout_holds 0 rows). **lh-money-escrow review 2026-10-04: CHANGES REQUIRED, all fixed (re-review pending):** the migration attaches the Q807 unconfirmed-email gate; the re-pay lives in tip_<tipId>, never job_<id> (a job_ transfer would be an unrecorded job payout to payoutClaim and job money to the clawback; edgeTransfersCarryTransferGroup lists it as not job-scoped); before an 'owed' row is marked 'kept' or reversed again, Stripe's reversals of the transfer are listed and ours (metadata.tip_id) is adopted, so a reversal whose row update failed is never stranded nor refused after the key window; a held tip pages nothing while young and a WARNING after 14 days that says not to pay it by hand; re-taking a stale 'repaying' claim is pinned to the updated_at it was read at; a Helpr with no payout account waits (back to 'reversed', warning when old); StripeIdempotencyError is final ('failed' + critical), not transient; balance_insufficient waits instead of 'failed'; transfer.reversed recognises our own hold-back (tip_hold_redrives.transfer_id) and skips the 'Investigate' warning; **second review 2026-10-04 (fixed):** a zero-row 'reversed' write pages unless another copy already moved the row; a row that becomes 'kept' tells the Helpr (once); the sweep's refused-reversal page names the tip, not '(sweep)'; idempotency_key_in_use is transient; held_money_not_redriven measures a tip from created_at ('owed') or first_repay_attempt_at; the short-balance age warning says to top up the platform balance; money-reconciliation's new held_money_not_redriven (critical) reports a refused row, or a clear Helpr's row still unpaid 24h after its last touch. ON DELETE RESTRICT on tip_hold_redrives.tip_id: a job delete that cascades to a held tip fails (23503) until a person settles it; checked: purge_old_seed_data already skips any job with a tips row (its v_refs list), account deletion anonymises jobs instead of deleting them, and the only tips delete (auto-tip-charge's claim release) runs before any payment, so no row can exist. done-when: sql `SELECT (to_regclass('public.tip_hold_redrives') IS NOT NULL)::text` => true **STATUS 2026-10-04 (money lane): NOT fixed: the obvious fix breaks an owner rule, and the other two need a design decision.** Built and then reverted: refunding a tip paid to a held Helpr from checkout.session.completed (reverse_transfer + refund_application_fee). It turned src/test/tipsAreFinal.test.ts red: Q781 (owner 2026-09-27) says tips are final and no code path refunds one. That leaves two ways out, and both need a mechanism the code does not have: (a) expire a held Helpr's open tip Checkout sessions when the hold is placed. admin_set_payout_hold is a DB RPC called from the admin client, so a Stripe call there needs an edge hook or a sweep, and a sweep still leaves a window until it runs. (b) On a paid tip for a held Helpr, reverse the transfer (the money goes back to the platform, not to the poster) and re-pay it once the hold is released. That needs the same release-time re-drive Q1223 needs. Recommendation: one 'held money re-drive' mechanism (a release hook or a sweep in a money-moving cron) for Q1222 (b) and Q1223 together. That is the lead's or owner's call before it is built. Prod 2026-10-04: payout_holds has 0 rows. create-payment's tip is a destination charge (transfer_data at ~create-payment:1348): the hold is checked when the session is created, and Stripe moves the money to the Helpr whenever the poster pays, with no second check. - [x] **Q1223 MEDIUM A won chargeback's re-payment to a held Helpr is never re-run after the hold is released (lh-money-escrow review of Q764, 2026-10-04).** **STATUS 2026-10-04 later (money lane, lead's design call: a sweep in process-scheduled-payouts): BUILT, deploy pending; supersedes the STATUS below.** The clawback module moved to _shared/chargebackClawback.ts (refactor commit, no behaviour change) so process-scheduled-payouts can call it. repayClawback now marks a held Helpr's row (since the review: its own column chargeback_clawbacks.held_repay_owed_at, plus failure_reason = HELD_REPAY_MARKER; written only there, i.e. only on a WON dispute) and its page says the re-pay is automatic; process-scheduled-payouts runs redriveHeldClawbackRepays every run: each dispute with a marked row whose Helprs are all clear now is re-run through repayClawback itself (same 'repaying' claim, same adopt-or-create, same key clawback-repay-<dispute>-<transfer> as the webhook), so nothing is paid twice; a Helpr still held waits. Unreachable live today (payout_holds 0 rows). **lh-money-escrow review 2026-10-04: CHANGES REQUIRED, all fixed (re-review pending):** the debt is held_repay_owed_at (migration 20261004220059), so a later failure_reason (a transient repay_failed) can no longer erase it; it is cleared only on the re-pay or when Stripe refuses (critical page for a person); an unreadable hold marks it owed too (critical page); the sweep picks up stale 'repaying' rows (a run that died after the claim) and goes PER ROW (a held crew-mate no longer makes the clear one wait; repayClawback skips held rows quietly from the sweep); a held re-pay is a WARNING that says not to pay by hand (the sweep adopts only REPAY_SOURCE transfers, so a manual payment would be paid twice) plus a warning after 14 days; the job stays 'chargeback' until every row is re-paid, then the sweep returns it to 'released' by the webhook's CAS; StripeIdempotencyError is no longer transient in the clawback (it looped forever). money-reconciliation's held_money_not_redriven covers an owed row whose Helpr is clear and still unpaid 24h later. **Second lh-money-escrow review 2026-10-04: CHANGES REQUIRED (MUST-1 double pay), fixed:** the sweep re-pays ONLY the owed rows it read (a refused sibling handed to a person is never paid again, and keeps the job in 'chargeback'); every repay claim is a compare-and-set on status AND updated_at; Stripe's idempotency_key_in_use is transient; held_money_not_redriven measures from held_repay_first_attempt_at (updated_at moves every hourly run, so it never fired). Open: a clawback re-pay refused with balance_insufficient is still final, filed as Q1291. done-when: test src/test/edge/chargebackClawback.test.ts done-when: test src/test/edge/process-scheduled-payouts.test.ts done-when: sql `SELECT (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns WHERE table_schema = 'public' AND table_name = 'chargeback_clawbacks' AND column_name = 'held_repay_owed_at'))::text` => true **STATUS 2026-10-04 (money lane): NOT fixed. It needs a design call first, and it shares that call with Q1222.** Confirmed by grep (2026-10-04): repayClawback is called ONLY from stripe-webhook/handlers/chargeDisputeClosed.ts (~:239). A held Helpr's row stays 'reversed' with nothing that marks the re-pay as owed. The hold is released by the DB RPC admin_release_payout_hold, called from the admin client (usePayoutHolds.ts), so there is no server-side release hook that could reach Stripe. Building the re-drive takes three things: (1) a marker on the skipped chargeback_clawbacks row saying the re-pay is owed once the hold lifts (failure_reason, or a new status, which is a migration); (2) moving repayClawback (or its core) into _shared, because no edge function imports from another function's directory today, and functions-deploy would not redeploy the importer when stripe-webhook changes; (3) a money-moving caller that runs after release: either a sweep in a scheduled function (process-scheduled-payouts already moves money on a schedule) or an edge action the admin client calls right after admin_release_payout_hold. Recommendation: one 'held money re-drive' used by Q1223 and by Q1222 (b). Lead's call on (2) and (3) before building. Prod 2026-10-04: payout_holds has 0 rows, so this cannot happen today. stripe-webhook/handlers/_chargebackClawback.ts repayClawback skips a held Helpr (the row stays 'reversed' and pages "re-pay FAILED"); chargeDisputeClosed.ts (~:239) is the only caller and the event does not recur, so release of the hold never pays it back automatically. Done when releasing a hold re-drives owed clawback re-payments (or a sweep does), with a test that fails without it. **DONE 2026-10-05 (auto-tick, verified live): every done-when marker on this item read as expected on prod (src/test/edge/chargebackClawback.test.ts passes; src/test/edge/process-scheduled-payouts.test.ts passes; got true, want true).**

- [x] **Q1192 MEDIUM Quick Release and Quick Refund can overwrite a chargeback block that lands during their Stripe call (found by the lh-money-escrow re-review of Q449, 2026-10-03; code read only, NOT exercised).** supabase/functions/create-payment/index.ts admin_release_dispute flips the job to completed/released with .eq("status", "disputed") only (about line 2291), and admin_refund_dispute flips it to cancelled/refunded the same way. If charge.dispute.created sets payment_status 'chargeback' while the transfer (or refund) call is in flight, the flip writes 'released' (or 'refunded') over it: the created handler had BLOCKED the job instead of clawing back the transfer that just left, so a lost chargeback becomes a platform loss and the chargeback marker no longer holds the job. Fix: pin both flips on the payment_status read too (escrow/payout_pending), and on zero rows re-read and, when it is 'chargeback', page and run the clawback path rather than writing over it. Guard to add: an edge case per flip in src/test/edge/create-payment.test.ts asserting the payment_status filter, red on today's flips. **STATUS 2026-10-04 (lead):** built on cloud/q1192-q1193; lh-money-escrow review CHANGES REQUIRED (Quick Release's pin broke on our own transfer.created webhook; the full-refund ledger row recorded the running total); both fixed by the lead (release pins escrow/payout_pending/released, the row records the refund's own amount), merged onto main's Q454 gift restore. Left: re-review, land, deploy, then the done-when. STATUS 2026-10-04 (lead): LANDED in batch 2 (#2252) as 6e1bf43be, with the lh-money-escrow review's must-fixes applied by the lead (its Sensitive-Review trailer records it; no second review ran), deployed with functions-deploy 2026-10-04. done-when: test src/test/disputeFlipsPinPaymentStatus.test.ts done-when: test src/test/edge/create-payment.test.ts **DONE 2026-10-05 (auto-tick, verified live): every done-when marker on this item read as expected on prod (src/test/disputeFlipsPinPaymentStatus.test.ts passes; src/test/edge/create-payment.test.ts passes).**
- [x] **Q1193 MEDIUM charge.refunded reads charge.refunds, which a webhook's minimal Charge does not carry (found by the lh-money-escrow review of Q450, 2026-10-03; pre-existing, NOT verified live).** supabase/functions/stripe-webhook/handlers/chargeRefunded.ts:48 takes the refund from charge.refunds?.data?.[0]. Since API version 2022-11-15 a Charge no longer carries refunds, and webhook objects are never expanded (Stripe docs: 'Expansion with webhooks'), so if the live endpoint is on a newer version latestRefund is always undefined: a Dashboard refund then writes NO payment_refunds ledger row (full: a logged WARN; partial: silent) and an onboarding-fee correction is never recognised. Measured 2026-10-03: payment_refunds holds 128 rows (admin_refund_dispute 1, admin_refund_general 12, cancel_escrow 110, void_cancelled_payments 5) and 0 'stripe_dashboard' rows ever; the last 24h of logs hold no charge.refunded delivery. Not measured: the live endpoint's API version (stripe-webhook-config-check returns only {id, url, status, livemode, enabled_events}; adding api_version there answers it with no new Stripe call). Fix when confirmed: list the charge's refunds (stripe.refunds.list({ charge }), as Q450's close already does) instead of reading the event's charge.refunds. Guard to add: an edge case in src/test/edge/disputeSettlementWebhook.test.ts with refunds: null that still writes the ledger row. **STATUS 2026-10-04 (lead):** built on cloud/q1192-q1193; lh-money-escrow review CHANGES REQUIRED (Quick Release's pin broke on our own transfer.created webhook; the full-refund ledger row recorded the running total); both fixed by the lead (release pins escrow/payout_pending/released, the row records the refund's own amount), merged onto main's Q454 gift restore. - [x] **Q1196 LOW src/test/hireColumnsRpcOnly.test.ts layer 3 reads `helper_id: null` as a hire write (found by the Q1187 lane, 2026-10-03).** Its FORBIDDEN pattern is /helper_id\s*:\s*(?!null\b)/: the second \s* backtracks to nothing and the lookahead then sees " null", so a client unassign written with a space is flagged (measured: true for "helper_id: null", false for "helper_id:null"). It errs toward red and no client jobs write unassigns today, so it has never fired. Fix: /helper_id\s*:(?!\s*null\b)/ and a case that pins it. **DONE 2026-10-04: the lookahead now holds the whitespace (`helper_id\s*:(?!\s*null\b)`), so an unassign with or without a space is not a hire write.** GUARD: src/test/hireColumnsRpcOnly.test.ts 'an unassign is not a hire write, with or without a space (Q1196)' (fails on the old pattern).

- [x] **Q1174 LOW The service worker caches signed-in Supabase responses (found by the Q1158 lab work, 2026-10-03).** vite.config.ts runtimeCaching routes every `https://*.supabase.co/*` GET through Workbox NetworkFirst into Cache Storage `api-cache` (50 entries, 300 s): the signed-in user's rows (profile, applications, messages) are written to disk on every load and every read is proxied through the SW. Sign-out (src/lib/authSignOut.ts) wipes the React Query memory and IndexedDB caches and the proof-photo sign cache, but not Cache Storage, so those responses stay on disk until Workbox expires them (read in source 2026-10-03; not run on a device). Not a measured slowdown (the lab blocks service workers); a privacy question first. Needs an lh-authz-rls look: drop the API route (NetworkOnly) or scope it to anon reads, and clear `api-cache` on sign-out. **2026-10-03 (lead; the same cache found from the e2e side by the Q1164 lane, filed there as a duplicate and folded in here):** 50 entries, 300 s, and nobody has checked that sign-out clears it, so a shared device could show the previous account's data offline. Check the sign-out path (WebKit and Chromium), fix if it survives, guard. **FIXED 2026-10-04 (cloud branch cloud/q1159-q1174; not merged or deployed yet).** vite.config.ts no longer has a rule for https://*.supabase.co/* (a request no rule matches goes straight to the network and is never stored), so the route is dropped rather than scoped; src/lib/apiCachePurge.ts deletes the legacy `api-cache` at sign-out (src/lib/authSignOut.ts, before the calls that can throw) and once per production page load (src/main.tsx), because Workbox only evicts when a rule writes, so devices that already hold it would keep it forever. public/sw.js (Capacitor shell worker) already passes everything else to the network. Not run on a device or in WebKit/Chromium: verified by source, by behaviour tests with a stubbed Cache Storage, and by the emitted dist/sw.js (see the PR). GUARD: src/test/swRuntimeCachingNeverStoresApi.test.ts (pins the whole runtimeCaching list, two-way; red on main's vite.config.ts, 3 failed; all 3 @mutate registrations red) and src/lib/authSignOut.test.ts (sign-out deletes api-cache; red on main's authSignOut.ts, 2 failed). **STATUS 2026-10-04 (lead): landed in the 2026-10-04 batch 2, reviewed; tick after its deploy is verified live.** **DONE 2026-10-04 (lead, open-list accuracy check): landed in batch 2 (#2252) as 3686c2b4a and deployed with the 2026-10-04 web prod deploy; guards src/test/swRuntimeCachingNeverStoresApi.test.ts and src/lib/authSignOut.test.ts.**

- [x] **Q1203 MEDIUM two edge functions compared a Louisiana date with the UTC date (found 2026-10-03).** `create-payment` (recurring_visit) refused a visit when `visit_date <= new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)` and measured the time left to the visit from UTC midnight; `charge-recurring-visits` had its own `todayUtc()`. From 19:00 CDT the UTC date is tomorrow's, so a visit's payment window closed the evening before it. FIXED: ONE helper, `louisianaToday()` (`supabase/functions/_shared/louisianaDate.ts`, America/Chicago; `accountPurge.ts`'s private copy now uses it), in both functions; create-payment also takes the visit's start from `jobLocalMidnightMs` instead of `T00:00:00Z`, so Checkout expires at the visit's Louisiana midnight.
